Combined with a noise-robust imputation strategy, EgoForce progressively generates stable and coherent full-body motion under strict causal constraints. Experiments demonstrate that our online framework outperforms existing online and offline methods, enabling long-horizon, full-body motion reconstruction in challenging egocentric scenarios.

</details>


### [37] [Revealing the Gap in Human and VLM Scene Perception through Counterfactual Semantic Saliency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13047)
*Ziqi Wen,Parsa Madinei,Miguel P. Eckstein*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Counterfactual Semantic Saliency (CSS)框架，通过因果消融物体并测量语义偏移来量化视觉语言模型(VLM)中物体的重要性。基于16,289条人类心理物理实验数据，发现VLM与人类存在显著的场景理解差异：模型过度依赖大物体、中心物体和高显著性物体，而对人物的依赖不足。模型的大小偏差是驱动模型-人类语义差异的主要因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的白盒可解释性方法无法适用于闭源架构的视觉语言模型，而被动评估指标无法隔离因果特征。因此需要一种黑盒、模型无关的框架来量化VLM与人类在高层语义场景理解上的一致性。

Method: 提出Counterfactual Semantic Saliency (CSS)框架，通过对场景中物体进行因果消融（移除），测量由此引发的语义偏移程度来量化物体的重要性。将CSS应用于多个主流VLM，并与基于16,289条人类心理物理实验数据的基线进行对比。

Result: VLM与人类存在普遍的语义理解差距：模型相对于人类更依赖大物体（大小偏差）、图像中心物体（中心偏差）和高显著性物体；同时模型对人物的依赖程度低于人类参与者。

Conclusion: 模型的大小偏差是解释模型与人类语义差异的主要驱动因素。CSS框架有效揭示了VLM在高层场景理解中与人类的对齐差距。

Abstract: Evaluating whether large vision-language models (VLMs) align with human perception for high-level semantic scene comprehension remains a challenge. Traditional white-box interpretability methods are inapplicable to closed-source architectures and passive metrics fail to isolate causal features. We introduce Counterfactual Semantic Saliency (CSS). This black-box, model-agnostic framework quantifies the importance of objects by measuring the semantic shift induced by their causal ablation from a scene. To evaluate AI-human semantic alignment, we tested prominent VLMs against a human psychophysics baseline comprising 16,289 valid responses across 307 complex natural scenes and 1,306 high-fidelity counterfactual variants. Our analysis reveals a pervasive scene comprehension gap: models exhibit an overreliance (relative to humans) on large objects (size bias), objects at the center of the image (center bias), and high saliency objects. In contrast, models rely less on people in the scenes than our human participants to describe the images. A model's size bias is a primary driver explaining variations in model-human semantic divergence. Code and data will be available at https://github.com/starsky77/Counterfactual-Semantic-Saliency.

</details>


### [38] [Uncertainty-aware Spatial-Frequency Registration and Fusion for Infrared and Visible Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13049)
*Xingyuan Li,Haoyuan Xu,Xingyue Zhu,Jun Ma,Yang Zou,Zhiying Jiang,Jinyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对红外与可见光图像融合中未配准条件下的累积误差问题，提出SFRF框架，通过多尺度迭代配准中的不确定性估计、频域热辐射分布一致性约束以及双支路空间-频率融合模块，实现鲁棒的配准与融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有未配准条件下的红外与可见光图像融合方法采用粗到细或跨尺度变形场估计进行配准，但忽视了配准中的累积误差，这会污染融合阶段并严重劣化最终图像质量。

Method: 1) 多尺度迭代配准（MIR）：跨尺度迭代优化变形场，每阶段引入不确定性估计以动态减轻误差累积；2) 热辐射分布一致性：在频域作为监督信号，确保配准中红外热分布全局一致；3) 双支路空间-频率融合（DSFF）：结合空间几何特征与频率分布信息重建图像。

Result: 在多个数据集上取得了令人印象深刻的性能表现。

Conclusion: SFRF通过将不确定性估计与频域一致性引入配准-融合统一流程，有效缓解了未配准条件下的误差累积问题，实现了空间与频率域上的鲁棒配准与融合。

Abstract: Infrared and Visible Image Fusion (IVIF) has shown promise in visual tasks under challenging environments, but fusion under unregistered conditions faces inherent misalignments. Current studies to solve them either predict the deformation parameters coarse-to-fine (i.e., coarse registration and fine registration) or estimate the deformation fields in multi-scales for registration. Though straightforward, they overlook the cumulative errors in registration, which contaminate the fusion stage and severely deteriorate the resulting images. We introduce the Spatial-Frequency Registration and Fusion (SFRF) framework, which incorporates uncertainty estimation and infrared thermal radiation distribution consistency into a unified pipeline to handle the error accumulation for robust registration and fusion across both spatial and frequency domains. Specifically, SFRF constructs a Multi-scale Iterative Registration (MIR) framework that iteratively refines the deformation field across scales, leveraging uncertainty estimation at each stage to mitigate error accumulation and enhance alignment accuracy dynamically. To ensure the accurate alignment of infrared thermal distributions during registration, thermal radiation distribution consistency is employed as a frequency-domain supervisory signal, promoting global consistency in the frequency domain. Based on the spatial-frequency alignment, SFRF further adopts a Dual-branch Spatial-Frequency Fusion (DSFF) module, which incorporates spatial geometric features and frequency distribution information to reconstruct visually appealing images. SFRF achieves impressive performance across diverse datasets.

</details>


### [39] [BrainAnytime: Anatomy-Aware Cross-Modal Pretraining for Brain Image Analysis with Arbitrary Modality Availability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13059)
*Guangqian Yang,Tong Ding,Wenlong Hou,Yue Xun,Ye Du,Qian Niu,Shujun Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BrainAnytime是一个统一的预训练框架，利用34,899个3D脑部扫描数据，支持任意模态组合（从单序列MRI到多模态PET）下的脑图像分析，通过跨模态蒸馏和解剖图谱引导的掩码策略，在多项下游任务中超越现有基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床诊断工作流程遵循模态递进路径，患者通常只有不完整且异质的模态子集，而现有AI模型大多假设固定的输入模态，无法灵活处理任意模态可用性的实际临床场景。

Method: 提出BrainAnytime统一预训练框架：(1) 基于34,899个3D脑扫描的5个数据集进行预训练；(2) 通过跨模态蒸馏（RCMD）学习MRI与PET之间的结构-分子对应关系；(3) 采用解剖图谱引导的课程掩码（PACM）优先关注疾病易感解剖区域；(4) 所有训练在共享的3D掩码自编码器（Multi-MAE3D）中完成。

Result: 在4个下游任务和5个临床驱动的模态设置中，BrainAnytime在大多数模态设置下显著优于模态特定模型、缺失模态基线和大规模脑MRI预训练基础模型。在CN vs. AD和CN vs. MCI分类中，平均准确率相对提升6.2%和7.0%。

Conclusion: BrainAnytime成功实现了在任意模态可用性下的统一脑图像分析，通过预训练学习跨模态对应关系并聚焦疾病相关解剖区域，为临床灵活的多模态诊断提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Clinical diagnostic workups typically follow a modality escalation pathway: after initial clinical evaluation, clinicians begin with routine structural imaging (e.g., MRI), selectively add sequences such as FLAIR or T2 to refine the differential, and reserve molecular imaging (e.g., amyloid-PET) for cases that remain uncertain after standard evaluation. Consequently, patients are observed with heterogeneous and often incomplete modality subsets. However, most current AI models assume fixed data modalities as the model inputs. In this paper, we present BrainAnytime, a unified pretraining framework pretrained on 34,899 3D brain scans from five datasets that support brain image analysis under arbitrary modality availability spanning multi-sequence MRI and amyloid-PET. A single model accepts whatever imaging is available, from a lone T1 scan to a full multimodal workup. Pretraining learns structural-molecular correspondences between MRI and PET via cross-modal distillation (RCMD) and prioritizes disease-vulnerable anatomy via atlas-guided curriculum masking (PACM), all within a shared 3D masked autoencoder (Multi-MAE3D). Across four downstream tasks and five clinically motivated modality settings, BrainAnytime largely outperforms modality-specific models, missing-modality baselines, and large-scale brain MRI pretrained foundation models on most modality settings. Notably, it surpasses the strongest missing-modality baselines with relative improvements of 6.2% and 7.0% in average accuracy on CN vs. AD and CN vs. MCI classification, respectively. Code is available at https://github.com/SDH-Lab/BrainAnytime.

</details>


### [40] [Edit-Compass & EditReward-Compass: A Unified Benchmark for Image Editing and Reward Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13062)
*Xuehai Bai,Yang Shi,Yi-Fan Zhang,Xuanyu Zhu,Yuran Wang,Yifan Dai,Xinyu Liu,Yiyan Ji,Xiaoling Gu,Yuanxing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Edit-Compass和EditReward-Compass，一个统一的图像编辑与奖励模型评估套件。Edit-Compass包含2,388个精细标注实例，覆盖6个难度递进的任务类别（如世界知识推理、视觉推理、多图像编辑），并采用基于结构化推理的细粒度多维评估框架；EditReward-Compass包含2,251个偏好对，模拟强化学习优化中的真实奖励建模场景。该工作旨在解决现有图像编辑基准任务难度有限、评估粒度粗，以及奖励模型基准脱离实际RL场景的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像编辑模型评估基准存在两个主要问题：1）任务难度有限且评估粒度粗，无法忠实反映人类判断，尤其对强模型区分度不足；2）奖励模型基准采用不切实际的评估设置，偏离实际强化学习（RL）优化场景，导致对图像编辑模型和奖励模型的可靠评估受限。

Method: 1) 构建Edit-Compass基准：包含2,388个精心标注的实例，覆盖6个难度递进的任务类别（世界知识推理、视觉推理、多图像编辑等），采用基于结构化推理的细粒度多维评分体系进行评估。2) 构建EditReward-Compass基准：包含2,251个偏好对，设计模拟RL优化过程中真实奖励建模场景的评估设置。

Result: 提出了一个包含2,388个标注实例和6类递进任务的图像编辑评估基准Edit-Compass，以及一个包含2,251个偏好对的奖励模型评估基准EditReward-Compass。

Conclusion: 通过构建统一评估套件Edit-Compass和EditReward-Compass，解决了现有图像编辑模型和奖励模型评估中任务难度有限、评估粒度粗以及脱离实际RL场景等关键问题，实现了对图像编辑模型和奖励模型的更可靠评估。

Abstract: Recent image editing models have achieved remarkable progress in instruction following, multimodal understanding, and complex visual editing. However, existing benchmarks often fail to faithfully reflect human judgment, especially for strong frontier models, due to limited task difficulty and coarse-grained evaluation protocols. In parallel, reward models have become increasingly important for RL-based image editing optimization, yet existing reward model benchmarks still rely on unrealistic evaluation settings that deviate from practical RL scenarios. These limitations hinder reliable assessment of both image editing models and reward models. To address these challenges, we introduce Edit-Compass and EditReward-Compass, a unified evaluation suite for image editing and reward modeling. Edit-Compass contains 2,388 carefully annotated instances spanning six progressively challenging task categories, covering capabilities such as world knowledge reasoning, visual reasoning, and multi-image editing. Beyond broad task coverage, Edit-Compass adopts a fine-grained multidimensional evaluation framework based on structured reasoning and carefully designed scoring rubrics. In parallel, EditReward-Compass contains 2,251 preference pairs that simulate realistic reward modeling scenarios during RL optimization.

</details>


### [41] [HarmoGS: Robust 3D Gaussian Splatting in the Wild via Conflict-Aware Gradient Harmonization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13073)
*Yulei Kang,Tianze Zhu,Jian-Fang Hu,Jianhuang Lai,Wei-Shi Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种冲突感知的3D高斯泼溅框架，通过语义一致性引导的掩码学习和双视图冲突感知梯度协调策略，解决了户外场景中瞬态干扰和光照不一致导致的渲染伪影问题，在多个基准上达到SOTA渲染质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图像级掩码的方法无法完全消除残留遮挡和光照引起的不一致性，导致矛盾的多视图梯度破坏高斯优化稳定性，产生可见的重建伪影。

Method: 1) 语义一致性引导掩码学习：逐像素计算一致性分数，自适应优化先验掩码，抑制不可靠监督；2) 双视图冲突感知梯度协调：将视角特定梯度相互旋转到正交配置，减少跨视图负向方向干扰；3) 冲突感知的稠密化和剪枝：稳定高斯生长并移除持续冲突的图元。

Result: 在多个户外场景标准基准上实现了最先进的渲染质量，有效应对复杂瞬态干扰和跨视图不一致。

Conclusion: 从图像空间监督和梯度级优化两个层面联合解决跨视图冲突，为户外3DGS场景下的鲁棒重建提供了有效方案。

Abstract: In-the-wild 3D Gaussian Splatting remains challenging due to transient distractors and illumination-induced cross-view appearance inconsistencies. Existing methods mainly rely on image-level masking to suppress unreliable supervision, but masking alone cannot fully eliminate residual occlusions or resolve illumination-induced inconsistencies, both of which can introduce conflicting cross-view gradients. These unresolved conflicts may destabilize Gaussian optimization and lead to visible reconstruction artifacts. We propose a conflict-aware 3DGS framework that addresses this problem from both image-space supervision and gradient-level optimization. Semantic Consistency-Guided Masking learns pixel-wise consistency scores to adaptively refine prior masks and suppress unreliable supervision before gradient formation. A dual-view Conflict-Aware Gradient Harmonization strategy further reconciles view-specific gradients by mutually rotating them into an orthogonal configuration, reducing negative directional interference across views. We also introduce conflict-aware densification and pruning to stabilize Gaussian growth and remove persistently conflicting primitives. Extensive experiments on standard in-the-wild benchmarks demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art rendering quality under complex transient distractors and cross-view inconsistencies.

</details>


### [42] [Learning to See What You Need: Gaze Attention for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13080)
*Junha Song,Byeongho Heo,Geonmo Gu,Jaegul Choo,Dongyoon Han,Sangdoo Yun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Gaze Attention机制，通过将视觉嵌入分组为紧凑的注视区域并动态选择相关区域进行注意力计算，使多模态大模型在生成时减少高达90%的视觉KV条目使用，同时保持或超越密集注意力基线的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态大模型在生成每个token时对所有视觉token进行均匀注意力，导致注意力分散和不必要的计算开销，缺乏人类视觉描述时选择性注视关键区域的能力。

Method: 1) 将视觉嵌入（以KV缓存形式存储）按空间分组为紧凑的注视区域，每个区域由轻量级描述符表示；2) 在每个解码步骤动态选择最相关的区域并限制注意力范围；3) 提出可学习的上下文token附加到每个图像或帧，以缓解局部注意力造成的全局上下文损失。

Result: 在图像和视频理解基准测试中，Gaze Attention匹配或超越了密集注意力基线，同时在注意力计算中使用的视觉KV条目减少了高达90%。

Conclusion: 通过选择性视觉注意力机制，可以在大幅降低计算开销的同时维持甚至提升多模态大模型的生成质量，验证了稀疏注意力在视觉语言任务中的有效性。

Abstract: When humans describe a visual scene, they do not process the entire image uniformly; instead, they selectively fixate on regions relevant to their intended description. In contrast, current multimodal large language models (MLLMs) attend to all visual tokens at each generation step, leading to diluted focus and unnecessary computational overhead. In this work, we introduce Gaze Attention, a novel mechanism that enables MLLMs to selectively attend to task-relevant visual regions during generation. Specifically, we spatially group visual embeddings-stored as key-value caches-into compact gaze regions, each represented by a lightweight descriptor. At each decoding step, the model dynamically selects the most relevant regions and restricts attention to them, reducing redundant computation while enhancing focus. To mitigate the loss of global context caused by localized attention, we further propose learnable context tokens appended to each image or frame, allowing the model to maintain holistic visual awareness. Extensive experiments on image and video understanding benchmarks demonstrate that Gaze Attention matches or surpasses dense-attention baselines, while using up to 90% fewer visual KV entries in the attention computation.

</details>


### [43] [PRA-PoE: Robust Alzheimer's Diagnosis with Arbitrary Missing Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13081)
*Guangqian Yang,Ye Du,Wenlong Hou,Qian Niu,Shujun Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PRA-PoE框架，通过原型锚定表示对齐（PRA）和不确定性感知专家乘积融合（UA-PoE），解决多模态学习中模态缺失时的表示偏移和不确定性校准问题，在AD评估任务中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理模态缺失时，隐式插补或合成特征无法显式建模模态可用性与不确定性，导致过度依赖合成特征、鲁棒性差以及不确定性估计失准。

Method: PRA-PoE包含两个核心组件：1) 原型锚定表示对齐（PRA）——利用可学习的全局原型和条件化令牌编码模态可用性，区分观察/缺失模态，重合成缺失特征并自适应精炼观察表示，以减少不同缺失模式下的表示偏移；2) 不确定性感知专家乘积融合（UA-PoE）——将每个模态建模为高斯专家，通过闭式专家乘积融合，高不确定性专家因低精度自动降低权重，提升不确定性可靠性。

Result: 在ADNI上平均准确率相对提升5.4%，在OASIS-3上平均F1相对提升10.9%，在所有非空模态子集上持续优于SOTA。

Conclusion: PRA-PoE能有效处理临床现实中模态缺失模式不匹配问题，提升多模态学习在AD评估中的鲁棒性和不确定性校准能力。

Abstract: Missing modalities are prevalent in real-world Alzheimer's disease (AD) assessment and pose a significant challenge to multimodal learning, particularly when the distribution of observed modality subsets differs between training and deployment. Such missingness pattern mismatch induces a conditional representation shift across modality subsets. Existing approaches that rely on implicit imputation or modality synthesis often fail to explicitly model modality availability and uncertainty, leading to overconfident dependence on synthesized features, reduced robustness, and miscalibrated uncertainty estimates. To address these limitations, we propose PRA-PoE, an incomplete multimodal learning framework that is equipped with Prototype-anchored Representation Alignment (PRA) and an Uncertainty-aware Product of Experts (UA-PoE) fusion mechanism. First, PRA uses learnable global prototypes and availability-conditioned tokens to encode modality availability, distinguish observed from missing modalities, re-synthesize features for missing modalities, and adaptively refine observed representations to align latent spaces across modality subsets, with the goal of reducing representation shift under varying missingness patterns. Second, UA-PoE models each modality as a Gaussian expert and performs closed-form Product of Experts fusion, where experts with higher uncertainty are automatically down-weighted via lower precision, improving uncertainty reliability. We evaluate PRA-PoE under a clinically realistic protocol by training with naturally missing data and testing on all non-empty modality combinations. PRA-PoE consistently outperforms the state-of-the-art across datasets, achieving a 5.4% relative improvement in average accuracy on ADNI and a 10.9% relative gain in average F1 on OASIS-3 over the strongest baseline across all non-empty modality subsets.

</details>


### [44] [RoSplat: Robust Feed-Forward Pixel-wise Gaussian Splatting for Varying Input Views and High-Resolution Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13093)
*Hoang Chuong Nguyen,Renjie Wu,Jose M. Alvarez,Miaomiao Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对可泛化3D高斯泼溅方法在输入视图数量变化时出现的过亮渲染问题和高分辨率渲染中的空洞伪影问题，提出了alpha归一化策略和3D采样正则化器，有效提升了多视图和高分辨率下的渲染质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于像素的前馈式可泛化3D高斯泼溅方法存在两个问题：(1) 推理时输入视图数量变化导致过亮渲染；(2) 高斯尺度估计缺乏充分监督，在高分辨率渲染中产生空洞伪影。本文旨在解决这些问题。

Method: (1) 提出alpha归一化策略，通过维持不同输入视图数量下的亮度一致性，解决过亮问题；(2) 引入基于3D采样的辅助正则化器，改进高斯尺度估计，减少高分辨率渲染中的空洞伪影。

Result: 在基准数据集上的实验表明，该方法在输入视图数量变化和高分辨率渲染场景下显著提升了基线模型的性能。

Conclusion: alpha归一化和3D采样正则化能有效提升可泛化3D高斯泼溅在变动输入视图和高分辨率设置下的鲁棒性和渲染质量。

Abstract: Generalizable 3D Gaussian Splatting has recently emerged as an efficient approach for novel-view synthesis, enabling feed-forward synthesis from only a few input views. However, existing pixel-wise feed-forward methods suffer from over-bright renderings when the number of input views varies during inference, as well as insufficient supervision for accurate Gaussian scale estimation, which leads to hole artifacts, particularly in high-resolution renderings. To address these issues, we identify that the over-brightness is caused by the varying number of overlapping Gaussians and propose a simple alpha normalization strategy to maintain brightness consistency across different number of input views. In addition, we introduce an auxiliary 3D sampling-based regularizer to improve Gaussian scale estimation, thereby mitigating hole artifacts in high-resolution rendering. Experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate that our method significantly improves baseline models under varying input-view and high-resolution rendering settings.

</details>


### [45] [Pyramid Forcing: Head-Aware Pyramid KV Cache Policy for High-Quality Long Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13111)
*Jiayu Chen,Junbei Tang,Wenbiao Zhao,Maoliang Li,Jiayi Luo,Zihao Zheng,Jiawei Yang,Guojie Luo,Xiang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Pyramid Forcing，一种基于头类型感知的分层KVCache框架，通过识别三种注意力头类型（Anchor、Wave、Veil）并分配不同缓存策略，有效提升自回归视频生成的长程质量，在VBench-Long上60秒评分从77.87提升至81.21。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归视频生成中KVCache策略通常对所有注意力头采用统一的历史帧保留方式，忽略了不同注意力头对历史依赖的异质性，导致长程生成时错误累积和视频质量退化。

Method: 1. 重新审视历史帧注意力，发现三种头类型：Anchor Head（需要广泛长程上下文）、Wave Head（周期性时间依赖）、Veil Head（关注初始和邻近帧）；2. 提出Pyramid Forcing框架：离线识别头类型，为每种头分配行为特定的缓存策略，通过高效的ragged-cache attention支持异构缓存长度。

Result: 在Self Forcing和Causal Forcing两种范式下，Pyramid Forcing在VBench-Long基准上持续提升长时生成质量：Self Forcing 60秒评分从77.87提升至81.21，同时在运动动态、视觉保真度和语义一致性方面均有改善。

Conclusion: Pyramid Forcing通过头感知的分层KV缓存策略，有效缓解了自回归视频生成的长程退化问题，验证了不同注意力头具有不同历史依赖模式的洞见。

Abstract: Autoregressive video generation enables streaming and open-ended long video synthesis, but still suffers from long-term degradation caused by accumulated errors. Existing KVCache strategies usually apply unified historical-frame retention, implicitly assuming homogeneous historical dependencies across attention heads. We revisit historical-frame attention and reveal three distinct head types: Anchor Heads require broad long-range context, Wave Heads exhibit periodic temporal dependencies, and Veil Heads focus on initial and adjacent frames. Based on this finding, we propose Pyramid Forcing, a head-aware pyramidal KVCache framework that identifies head types offline, assigns behavior-specific cache policies, and supports heterogeneous cache lengths via efficient ragged-cache attention. Experiments on Self Forcing and Causal Forcing show that Pyramid Forcing consistently improves long-horizon generation quality on VBench-Long, increasing the 60-second Self Forcing score from 77.87 to 81.21 while enhancing motion dynamics, visual fidelity, and semantic consistency. Project: https://if-lab-pku.github.io/Pyramid-Forcing/.

</details>


### [46] [Early Semantic Grounding in Image Editing Models for Zero-Shot Referring Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13122)
*Jingxuan He,Xiyu Wang,Yunke Wang,Mengyu Zheng,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现基于指令的图像编辑（IIE）模型在内部表征中隐式具备语言条件视觉语义定位能力，并据此提出一种无需训练、仅需单步去噪的框架，利用这些模型的中间表征实现零样本指代图像分割（RIS），在RefCOCO等数据集上超越现有零样本方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索基于指令的图像编辑模型内部隐式的语言条件视觉语义定位能力，挖掘其用于零样本指代图像分割的潜力，以解决现有RIS方法依赖大量标注数据的问题。

Method: 提出无需训练的框架，分解定位为两个互补组件：基于注意力的空间先验（估计聚焦区域）和基于特征的语义判别（确定分割对象），仅利用最早去噪时间步的中间表征，以单步去噪生成精确分割掩码，无需完整图像合成。

Result: 在RefCOCO、RefCOCO+和RefCOCOg数据集上，该方法在零样本设置下优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 图像编辑模型内部表征在最早去噪步即展现出强前景-背景可分离性，利用这一特性可有效实现零样本指代分割。

Abstract: Instruction-based image editing (IIE) models have recently demonstrated strong capability in modifying specific image regions according to natural language instructions, which implicitly requires identifying where an edit should be applied. This indicates that such models inherently perform language-conditioned visual semantic grounding. In this work, we investigate whether this implicit grounding can be leveraged for zero-shot referring image segmentation (RIS), a task that requires pixel-level localization of objects described by natural language expressions. Through systematic analysis, we reveal that strong foreground-background separability emerges in the internal representations of these models at the earliest denoising timestep, well before any visible image transformation occurs. Building on this insight, we propose a training-free framework that repurposes pretrained image editing models for RIS by exploiting their intermediate representations. Our approach decomposes localization into two complementary components: attention-based spatial priors that estimate where to focus, and feature-based semantic discrimination that determines what to segment. By leveraging feature-space separability, the framework produces accurate segmentation masks using only a single denoising step, without requiring full image synthesis. Extensive experiments on RefCOCO, RefCOCO+, and RefCOCOg demonstrate that our method achieves superior performance over existing zero-shot baselines.

</details>


### [47] [Multi-Modal Guided Multi-Source Domain Adaptation for Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13140)
*Sangin Lee,Seokjun Kwon,Jeongmin Shin,Namil Kim,Yukyung Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MS-DePro方法，通过引入深度图和文本这两种域无关模态，解决多源域适应目标检测中域特定与域无关特征难以分离的问题，在多个基准上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MSDA方法在处理多个源域的RGB图像时，难以从域特定的特征图中有效分离和保留域无关信息，导致跨域检测性能受限。

Method: MS-DePro包含两个核心模块：(1) 深度引导定位（depth-guided localization），利用深度图生成域无关的区域提案；(2) 多模态引导提示学习（multi-modal guided prompt learning），融合多模态特征对齐可学习的文本嵌入进行分类。

Result: 在多个MSDA基准测试上达到最先进水平，消融实验验证了各模块的有效性。

Conclusion: 引入深度图和文本等域无关模态可以有效解决多源域适应目标检测中的域特定/域无关特征分离难题，为领域自适应目标检测提供了新的思路。

Abstract: General object detection (OD) struggles to detect objects in the target domain that differ from the training distribution. To address this, recent studies demonstrate that training from multiple source domains and explicitly processing them separately for multi-source domain adaptation (MSDA) outperforms blending them for unsupervised domain adaptation (UDA). However, existing MSDA methods learn domain-agnostic features from domain-specific RGB images while preserving domain-specific information from the domain-agnostic feature map. To address this, we propose MS-DePro: Multi-Source Detector with Depth and Prompt, composed of (1) depth-guided localization and (2) multi-modal guided prompt learning. We leverage domain-agnostic input modalities, namely depth maps and text, to encode domain-agnostic characteristics. Specifically, we utilize depth maps to generate domain-agnostic region proposals for localization and integrate multi-modal features to align learnable text embeddings for classification. MS-DePro achieves state-of-the-art performance on MSDA benchmarks, and comprehensive ablations demonstrate the effectiveness of our contributions. Our code is available on https://github.com/sejong-rcv/Multi-Modal-Guided-Multi-Source-Domain-Adaptation-for-Object-Detection.

</details>


### [48] [GenCape: Structure-Inductive Generative Modeling for Category-Agnostic Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13151)
*Jiyong Rao,Yu Wang,Shengjie Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GenCape提出了一种基于生成式框架的类别无关姿态估计方法，通过结构感知变分自编码器（i-SVAE）和组合图迁移（CGT）模块，仅从图像支撑样本推断关键点关系，无需预定义骨架或文本描述，在MP-100数据集上显著超越基于图的支撑基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CAPE方法要么将关键点视为孤立实体，要么依赖手动定义的骨架先验，这些先验标注成本高且在不同类别间缺乏灵活性，限制了模型捕捉实例级结构线索的能力，从而影响像素级定位精度。

Method: 提出GenCape框架，包含两个核心组件：1) 迭代结构感知变分自编码器（i-SVAE），通过变分推理从支撑特征推断软性、实例特定的邻接矩阵，并逐层嵌入图Transformer解码器以渐进细化结构先验；2) 组合图迁移（CGT）模块，通过贝叶斯融合和注意力重加权将多个潜在图自适应聚合成查询感知结构，增强对视觉不确定性和支撑偏差的鲁棒性。

Result: 在MP-100数据集上，GenCape在1-shot和5-shot设置下均显著优于基于图的支撑基线方法，并与使用文本支撑的先进方法保持竞争性能。

Conclusion: 结构感知设计有助于在关键点间实现有效的消息传播，并促进跨不同关键点拓扑的目标类别间的语义对齐，验证了生成式框架在CAPE任务中的潜力。

Abstract: Category-agnostic pose estimation (CAPE) aims to localize keypoints on query images from arbitrary categories, using only a few annotated support examples for guidance. Recent approaches either treat keypoints as isolated entities or rely on manually defined skeleton priors, which are costly to annotate and inherently inflexible across diverse categories. Such oversimplification limits the model's capacity to capture instance-wise structural cues critical for accurate pixel-level localization. To overcome these limitations, we propose GenCape, a Generative-based framework for CAPE that infers keypoint relationships solely from image-based support inputs, without additional textual descriptions or predefined skeletons. Our framework consists of two principal components: an iterative Structure-aware Variational Autoencoder (i-SVAE) and a Compositional Graph Transfer (CGT) module. The former infers soft, instance-specific adjacency matrices from support features through variational inference, embedded layer-wise into the Graph Transformer Decoder for progressive structural priors refinement. The latter adaptively aggregates multiple latent graphs into a query-aware structure via Bayesian fusion and attention-based reweighting, enhancing resilience to visual uncertainty and support-induced bias. This structure-aware design facilitates effective message propagation among keypoints and promotes semantic alignment across object categories with diverse keypoint topologies. Experimental results on the MP-100 dataset show that our method achieves substantial gains over graph-support baselines under both 1- and 5-shot settings, while maintaining competitive performance against text-support counterparts.

</details>


### [49] [EvObj: Learning Evolving Object-centric Representations for 3D Instance Segmentation without Scene Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13152)
*Jiahao Chen,Zihui Zhang,Yafei Yang,Jinxi Li,Shenxing Wei,Zhixuan Sun,Bo Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EvObj是一种用于无监督3D实例分割的方法，通过两个创新模块（对象辨别模块和对象补全模块）弥合合成预训练数据和真实点云之间的几何域差距，在真实和合成数据集上均达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前方法在从合成数据集（如ShapeNet）向真实扫描数据（如ScanNet）迁移对象先验时存在结构差异，尤其是形态变化和遮挡伪影导致的几何域差距问题。

Method: 集成两个创新模块：（1）对象辨别模块，动态细化对象候选，实现对象先验对目标域的持续适应；（2）对象补全模块，在发现对象后重建部分几何形状。

Result: 在真实世界和合成数据集上的大量实验表明，EvObj在3D对象分割性能上优于所有基线方法，达到了最先进的结果。

Conclusion: EvObj有效解决了无监督3D实例分割中合成数据到真实数据的域迁移问题，通过动态适应和几何补全策略实现了跨域性能突破。

Abstract: We introduce EvObj for unsupervised 3D instance segmentation that bridges the geometric domain gap between synthetic pretraining data and real-world point clouds. Current methods suffer from structural discrepancies when transferring object priors from synthetic datasets (e.g., ShapeNet) to real scans (e.g., ScanNet), particularly due to morphological variations and occlusion artifacts. To address this, EvObj integrates two innovative modules: (1) An object discerning module that dynamically refines object candidates, enabling continuous adaptation of object priors to target domains; and (2) An object completion module that reconstructs partial geometries after discovering objects. We conduct extensive experiments on both real-world and synthetic datasets, demonstrating superior 3D object segmentation performance over all baselines while achieving state-of-the-art results.

</details>


### [50] [Pareto-Guided Optimal Transport for Multi-Reward Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13155)
*Ying Ba,Tianyu Zhang,Mohan Zhou,Yalong Bai,Wenyi Mo,Guiwei Zhang,Bing Su,Ji-Rong Wen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种帕累托前沿引导的最优传输（PG-OT）框架，通过构建与提示相关的帕累托前沿并利用分布感知的最优传输将劣势样本映射至前沿，有效解决了多奖励模型对齐中的奖励破解问题，在联合支配率和人工评估中分别取得11%和80%的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像生成模型在多奖励模型对齐中面临两大挑战：1）加权重加和的多奖励融合方法调优成本高且难以平衡冲突目标；2）优化过程极易遭受奖励破解（reward hacking），即奖励分数升高但感知图像质量下降。

Method: 提出PG-OT框架，核心包含：1）构建与提示相关的帕累托前沿；2）通过分布感知的最优传输（optimal transport）将劣势样本映射至帕累托前沿；3）针对不同奖励信号特性设计了在线和离线两种优化策略；4）提出联合支配率（JDR）和联合崩溃率（JCR）作为量化多奖励协同与奖励破解的评估指标。

Result: 在联合支配率（JDR）上相比强基线提升11%，人工评估中取得近80%的胜率。

Conclusion: PG-OT框架通过帕累托前沿引导的最优传输有效缓解了多奖励对齐中的奖励破解问题，为多目标偏好对齐提供了新的方法论，所提出的JDR和JCR指标也为更严格的奖励对齐评估提供了标准化工具。

Abstract: Text-to-image generation models have achieved remarkable progress in preference optimization, yet achieving robust alignment across diverse reward models remains a significant challenge. Existing multi-reward fusion approaches rely on weighted summation, which is costly to tune and insufficient for balancing conflicting objectives. More critically, optimization with reward models is highly susceptible to reward hacking, where reward scores increase while the perceived quality of generated images deteriorates. We demonstrate that optimizing against a unified global target under heterogeneous reward upper bounds can induce reward hacking, a risk further exacerbated by the inherent instability of weak reward models. To mitigate this, we propose a Pareto Frontier-Guided Optimal Transport (PG-OT) framework. Our method constructs a prompt-specific Pareto frontier and maps dominated samples toward it via distribution-aware optimal transport. Furthermore, we develop both online and offline optimization strategies tailored to diverse reward signal characteristics. To provide a more rigorous assessment, we introduce the Joint Domination Rate (JDR) and Joint Collapse Rate (JCR) as principled metrics to quantify multi-reward synergy and reward hacking. Experimental results show that our approach outperforms strong baselines with an 11% gain in JDR and achieves a near 80% win rate in human evaluations.

</details>


### [51] [Dual-Pathway Circuits of Object Hallucination in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13156)
*Jiaxin Liu,Ding Zhong,Yue Wang,Zhidong Yang,Zhaolu Kang,Guangyuan Dong,Qishi Zhan,Pengcheng Fang,Aofan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出双路径电路分析框架，用于识别和表征视觉语言模型中的物体幻觉相关神经回路。通过激活修补、条件路径分析和针对性抑制，发现视觉接地路径和幻觉路径的极性翻转机制，并实现高达76%的幻觉抑制率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型常产生物体幻觉（描述图像中不存在的物体），限制其可靠性和可解释性。现有方法缺乏对幻觉背后神经机制的深入理解。

Method: 1) 在5种架构不同的VLM上应用激活修补，识别视觉接地路径和幻觉路径；2) 提出条件路径分析(CPA)表征路径级交互，发现接地组件在正确和幻觉样本中维持强冗余但发生极性翻转；3) 对幻觉路径组件进行针对性抑制。

Result: 1) 抑制幻觉路径组件可减少高达76%的物体幻觉，且准确率损失极小；2) 相同电路选择性迁移到关系型幻觉但非属性型幻觉；3) 在POPE-adversarial和AMBER评估集上验证电路跨架构一致性、支持因果干预、跨幻觉类型选择性迁移。

Conclusion: VLM中的幻觉由可识别的神经回路驱动，通过理解这些回路的极性翻转机制可以有针对性进行干预，实现高效的幻觉抑制。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in bridging visual perception and natural language understanding, enabling a wide range of multimodal reasoning tasks. However, they often produce object hallucinations, describing content absent from the input image, which limits their reliability and interpretability. To address this limitation, we propose Dual-Pathway Circuit Analysis, a framework that identifies and characterizes hallucination-related circuits in VLMs for mechanistic understanding and causal probing. We first apply activation patching across five architecturally diverse VLMs to identify a visual grounding pathway that supports correct predictions and a hallucination pathway that drives erroneous outputs. We then introduce Conditional Pathway Analysis (CPA) to characterize pathway-level interactions, revealing that grounding components remain strongly redundant in both correct and hallucinating samples but undergo a consistent polarity flip, shifting from supporting the ground truth on correct samples to aligning with the hallucinated answer on erroneous ones. We further perform targeted suppression of hallucination-pathway components, showing that scaling these components reduces object hallucination by up to 76% with minimal accuracy cost, and validate that the same circuit selectively transfers to relational but not attribute hallucination. Evaluations on POPE-adversarial and AMBER show that the identified circuits are consistent across architectures, support causal intervention, and transfer selectively across hallucination types.

</details>


### [52] [A$_3$B$_2$: Adaptive Asymmetric Adapter for Alleviating Branch Bias in Vision-Language Image Classification with Few-Shot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13161)
*Yiyun Zhou,Zhonghua Jiang,Wenkang Han,Kunxi Li,Mingjing Xu,Chang Yao,Jingyuan Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示视觉-语言模型（如CLIP）在少样本图像分类中存在"分支偏置"问题：图像编码器适配在分布外场景下并非总是有益。为此提出A₃B₂——自适应非对称适配器，通过不确定性感知适配器阻尼（UAAD）自动抑制高不确定性时的图像分支适配，并采用轻量级非对称专家混合设计。在11个数据集上超越11个基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言模型迁移方法隐含假设图像和文本分支具有均匀重要性，但该假设在图像分类中未被系统研究。作者通过分析发现分支偏置问题，即图像编码器适配在分布外场景下可能损害性能，亟需一种自适应控制机制。

Method: 提出A₃B₂框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 不确定性感知适配器阻尼（UAAD），根据预测不确定性自动抑制图像分支适配强度；(2) 轻量级非对称适配器架构，借鉴专家混合思想并引入负载均衡正则化，实现无需手动干预的数据驱动控制。

Result: 在三个少样本图像分类任务、11个标准数据集上，A₃B₂一致性地超越了11种主流的基于提示和适配器的基线方法，验证了其在多种分布偏移场景下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 视觉-语言模型迁移中存在分支偏置现象，非对称自适应适配策略比对称微调更为有效。A₃B₂通过不确定性驱动的自适应机制，在保持计算效率的同时显著提升了分布外泛化能力。

Abstract: Efficient transfer learning methods for large-scale vision-language models ($e.g.$, CLIP) enable strong few-shot transfer, yet existing adaptation methods follow a fixed fine-tuning paradigm that implicitly assumes a uniform importance of the image and text branches, which has not been systematically studied in image classification. Through extensive analysis, we reveal a Branch Bias issue in vision-language image classification: adapting the image encoder does not always improve performance under out-of-distribution settings. Motivated by this observation, we propose A$_3$B$_2$, an Adaptive Asymmetric Adapter that alleviates Branch Bias in few-shot learning. A$_3$B$_2$ introduces Uncertainty-Aware Adapter Dampening (UAAD), which automatically suppresses image-branch adaptation when prediction uncertainty is high, enabling soft and data-driven control without manual intervention. Architecturally, A$_3$B$_2$ adopts a lightweight asymmetric design inspired by mixture-of-experts with Load Balancing Regularization. Extensive experiments on three few-shot image classification tasks across 11 datasets demonstrate that A$_3$B$_2$ consistently outperforms 11 competitive prompt- and adapter-based baselines.

</details>


### [53] [PanoWorld: Towards Spatial Supersensing in 360$^\circ$ Panorama World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13169)
*Changpeng Wang,Xin Lin,Junhan Liu,Yuheng Liu,Zhen Wang,Donglian Qi,Yunfeng Yan,Xi Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对多模态大模型在360度全景图像理解中的局限性，提出了全景原生理解范式，构建了PanoWorld模型（采用球面空间交叉注意力机制）和PanoSpace-Bench基准，通过几何感知的全景原生监督数据训练，在多个基准上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在空间理解上受限于视角图像范式，继承了人类感知的窄视野缺陷，无法充分利用360度全景图像提供的完整环境信息；同时现有方法通常将全景图分解为多个透视视图，导致球面结构信息（如ERP投影的几何特性）隐式丢失。

Method: 1) 定义了全景原生理解的关键能力：语义锚定、球面定位、参考系变换、深度感知的3D空间推理；2) 构建大规模元数据管线，将混合源ERP全景图转换为几何感知、语言接地、深度感知的监督信号，生成能力对齐的指令微调数据；3) 提出PanoWorld模型，设计球面空间交叉注意力机制，将球面几何注入视觉流；4) 构建PanoSpace-Bench诊断基准评估全景原生空间推理能力。

Result: PanoWorld在PanoSpace-Bench、H*Bench和R2R-CE Val-Unseen基准上均显著优于专有模型和开源基线模型。

Conclusion: 鲁棒的全景推理需要专用的全景原生监督数据和几何感知的模型适配，验证了球面几何注入对全景空间理解的关键作用。

Abstract: Multimodal large laboratory models (MLLMs) still struggle with spatial understanding under the dominant perspective-image paradigm, which inherits the narrow field of view of human-like perception. For navigation, robotic search, and 3D scene understanding, 360-degree panoramic sensing offers a form of supersensing by capturing the entire surrounding environment at once. However, existing MLLM pipelines typically decompose panoramas into multiple perspective views, leaving the spherical structure of equirectangular projection (ERP) largely implicit. In this paper, we study pano-native understanding, which requires an MLLM to reason over an ERP panorama as a continuous, observer-centered space. To this end, we first define the key abilities for pano-native understanding, including semantic anchoring, spherical localization, reference-frame transformation, and depth-aware 3D spatial reasoning. We then build a large-scale metadata construction pipeline that converts mixed-source ERP panoramas into geometry-aware, language-grounded, and depth-aware supervision, and instantiate these signals as capability-aligned instruction tuning data. On the model side, we introduce PanoWorld with Spherical Spatial Cross-Attention, which injects spherical geometry into the visual stream. We further construct PanoSpace-Bench, a diagnostic benchmark for evaluating ERP-native spatial reasoning. Experiments show that PanoWorld substantially outperforms both proprietary and open-source baselines on PanoSpace-Bench, H* Bench, and R2R-CE Val-Unseen benchmarks. These results demonstrate that robust panoramic reasoning requires dedicated pano-native supervision and geometry-aware model adaptation. All source code and proposed data will be publicly released.

</details>


### [54] [CLIP Tricks You: Training-free Token Pruning for Efficient Pixel Grounding in Large VIsion-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13178)
*Sangin Lee,Yukyung Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LiteLVLM是一种无需训练的、文本引导的视觉令牌剪枝策略，通过反转CLIP视觉-文本相似度排序，在像素级定位任务中高效保留关键令牌，实现22%速度提升和2.3倍内存减少，性能仅下降10%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大视觉语言模型中视觉令牌占据多数输入令牌，导致巨大计算开销。现有剪枝方法在像素级定位任务中表现不佳，因为令牌重要性高度依赖输入文本。CLIP分析发现，参考区域内的视觉令牌与文本表示的相似度往往很低，这启发作者提出新的剪枝策略。

Method: 提出LiteLVLM，一种无需训练的文本引导令牌剪枝策略。核心是通过反转CLIP视觉-文本相似度排序，保留覆盖参考区域的视觉令牌，同时恢复上下文令牌以实现清晰的前景-背景分离。

Result: 在不同令牌预算下，LiteLVLM比现有方法性能提升超过5%；无需任何训练或微调，保持90%原始性能的同时实现22%速度提升和2.3倍内存减少。

Conclusion: LiteLVLM有效解决了像素级定位任务中视觉令牌剪枝的难题，证明了无需训练的文本引导策略在大视觉语言模型中的高效性和实用性。

Abstract: In large vision-language models, visual tokens typically constitute the majority of input tokens, leading to substantial computational overhead. To address this, recent studies have explored pruning redundant or less informative visual tokens for image understanding tasks. However, these methods struggle with pixel grounding tasks, where token importance is highly contingent on the input text. Through an in-depth analysis of CLIP, we observe that visual tokens located within referent regions often exhibit low similarity to the textual representation. Motivated by this insight, we introduce LiteLVLM, a training-free, text-guided token pruning strategy for efficient pixel grounding inference. By reversing the ranking of CLIP's visual-text similarity, LiteLVLM effectively retains visual tokens covering the referent regions, while recovering context tokens to enable clear foreground-background separation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LiteLVLM significantly outperforms existing methods by over 5% across diverse token budgets. Without any training or fine-tuning, LiteLVLM maintains 90\% of the original performance with a 22% speedup and a 2.3x memory reduction. Our code is available at https://github.com/sejong-rcv/LiteLVLM.

</details>


### [55] [Does Engram Do Memory Retrieval in Autoregressive Image Generation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13179)
*Jinghao Wang,Qiyuan He,Chunbin Gu,Pheng-Ann Heng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统性地考察了Engram模块在自回归图像生成中的实际作用，发现该模块并非如其名义上的“内容寻址记忆”机制，而实质上是一个哈希键控的门控侧通路（gated architectural side-pathway），其性能提升主要来自通路结构本身而非记忆表的内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Engram模块在语言模型中表现出色并被解释为内容寻址的局部token模式快捷方式，本文希望验证这一解释是否迁移到自回归图像生成任务中，或者观察到的增益是否源于不同机制。

Method: 将Engram自适应到视觉任务（2D空间n-gram哈希、门控融合、KV-cache兼容增量推理），注入到基于ImageNet 256×256的类条件AR生成器中；通过门控钳位实验、供体探针实验、冻结记忆表为随机噪声等消融分析来探究模块的实际工作机制。

Result: 在所有骨干/记忆预算比ρ∈[0.17,0.90]下，Engram增强变体的FID均不如纯AR基线；禁用Engram通路会导致灾难性下降，但恒等小门控值（g=0.10）即可匹配或超越学习门控；哈希输入替换为匹配/对抗/随机同类样本时下一token分布无显著差异；冻结记忆表为N(0,1)噪声仅损失ΔFID=0.10且提升了IS。

Conclusion: Engram在AR图像生成中并非内容寻址检索器，而是一个哈希键控的门控架构侧通路，其收益主要由通路结构本身主导，学习到的记忆表仅贡献微小的分布精炼。

Abstract: The Engram module -- a hash-keyed, O(1) associative memory injected into Transformer layers -- was recently shown to improve large language model pretraining, with the appealing interpretation that it provides a content-addressed shortcut to recurring local token patterns. We ask whether this interpretation transfers to autoregressive (AR) image generation, or whether the observed gains, if any, come from a different mechanism. We adapt the Engram module to vision with 2D spatial $n$-gram hashing, gated fusion, and KV-cache-compatible incremental inference, and inject it into a class-conditional AR generator trained on ImageNet 256x256. Across a sweep of backbone-to-memory budget ratios $ρ{\in}[0.17, 0.90]$, every Engram-augmented variant trails the pure AR baseline in FID, indicating that the module saves backbone FLOPs but does not, by itself, improve sample quality. We then probe how the module is used. A gate-clamp sweep shows that disabling the Engram pathway entirely is catastrophic, yet a tiny constant gate (g=0.10) matches or beats the learned gate -- inconsistent with a heavily content-addressed recall mechanism. A donor-probe experiment shows that swapping the hash inputs for matched, adversarial, or random same-class exemplars produces statistically indistinguishable next-token distributions, while collapsing or randomising the table degrades them by two to three orders of magnitude. Finally, training a model from scratch with the entire memory table frozen to $\mathcal{N}(0, 1)$ noise costs only $Δ\text{FID}{=}0.10$ and actually raises Inception Score. Together, these findings indicate that the Engram in AR image generation behaves not as a content-addressed retriever but as a gated architectural side-pathway: a hash-keyed residual stream whose benefit is dominated by the pathway itself, with the learned table contributing only a small distributional refinement.

</details>


### [56] [DiffST: Spatiotemporal-Aware Diffusion for Real-World Space-Time Video Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13182)
*Zheng Chen,Ruofan Yang,Jin Han,Dehua Song,Zichen Zou,Chunming He,Yong Guo,Yulun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DiffST，一种高效的时空感知视频扩散框架，用于联合时空视频超分辨率（STVSR）。通过适配预训练扩散模型实现单步采样、直接处理整个视频，并引入跨帧上下文聚合（CFCA）和视频表示引导（VRG）模块，在保持高推理效率（比此前扩散方法快17倍）的同时取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型在视频超分辨率和帧插值中表现良好，但在联合时空超分辨率（STVSR）中应用有限，存在推理效率低和时空信息利用不充分两个问题，限制了实际部署。

Method: (1) 适配预训练扩散模型进行单步采样，直接处理整个视频而非逐帧操作，提升效率；(2) 提出跨帧上下文聚合（CFCA）模块，跨多个关键帧聚合信息生成中间帧；(3) 提出视频表示引导（VRG）模块，提取视频级全局特征指导扩散过程。

Result: 在真实世界STVSR任务上取得领先结果，推理速度比此前扩散方法快约17倍。

Conclusion: DiffST有效解决了扩散模型在STVSR中的效率和时空信息利用问题，为实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Diffusion-based models have shown strong performance in video super-resolution (VSR) and video frame interpolation (VFI). However, their role in the coupled space-time video super-resolution (STVSR) setting remains limited. Existing diffusion-based STVSR approaches suffer from two issues: (1) low inference efficiency and (2) insufficient utilization of spatiotemporal information. These limitations impede deployment. To address these issues, we introduce DiffST, an efficient spatiotemporal-aware video diffusion framework for real-world STVSR. To improve efficiency, we adapt a pre-trained diffusion model for one-step sampling and process the entire video directly rather than operating on individual frames. Furthermore, to enhance spatiotemporal information utilization, we introduce cross-frame context aggregation (CFCA) and video representation guidance (VRG). The CFCA module aggregates information across multiple keyframes to produce intermediate frames. The VRG module extracts video-level global features to guide the diffusion process. Extensive experiments show that DiffST obtains leading results on real-world STVSR tasks. It also maintains high inference efficiency, running about 17$\times$ faster than previous diffusion-based STVSR methods. Code is available at: https://github.com/zhengchen1999/DiffST.

</details>


### [57] [FIKA-Bench: From Fine-grained Recognition to Fine-Grained Knowledge Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13193)
*Geng Li,Yuxin Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FIKA-Bench基准，评估多模态模型在开放环境下通过主动搜索、验证外部证据进行细粒度知识获取的能力。最佳模型仅达25.1%准确率，且单纯配备工具不足以解决该挑战，主要失败原因在于实体检索错误和视觉判断不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要评估视觉识别能力，而人类在面对不熟悉物体时会主动搜索和验证外部证据，这种主动外部知识获取能力在现有研究中未被充分探索。

Method: 构建FIKA-Bench数据集，包含311个来源于公开资源和真实场景的实例，经过泄露感知过滤（排除前沿闭卷模型可回答的案例）和证据审核（消除图文泄露），仅保留有可验证证据支撑的样本。

Result: 最佳系统仅达25.1%准确率，无模型超过30%。单纯配备工具不足以弥合差距，失败主要源于实体检索错误和视觉判断不足。

Conclusion: 可靠的细粒度知识获取需要更好的智能体设计，专注于细粒度识别能力的提升。

Abstract: Fine-grained recognition in everyday life is often not a closed-book classification problem: when encountering unfamiliar objects, humans actively search, compare visual details, and verify evidence before deciding. Existing benchmarks primarily evaluate visually recognition, leaving this active external knowledge acquisition ability underexplored. We study fine-grained knowledge acquisition, where a system must seek, verify, and use external evidence to answer open-ended fine-grained recognition questions. We introduce FIKA-Bench, a leakage-aware and evidence-grounded collection of 311 public-source and real-life instances. To ensure high quality, every example is filtered against frontier closed-book models to remove memorized cases and audited to eliminate image-answer leakage, retaining only samples supported by verified evidence. Our evaluation of latest Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) and agents reveals that the task remains a formidable challenge: the best system reaches only 25.1% accuracy, with no model exceeding 30%. Crucially, we find that merely equipping models with tools is insufficient to bridge this gap; agent failures are predominantly driven by wrong entity retrieval and poor visual judgement. These results show that reliable knowledge acquisition needs better agent designs that focus on fine-grained recognition.

</details>


### [58] [STAR: Semantic-Temporal Adaptive Representation Learning for Few-Shot Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13202)
*Hongli Liu,Yu Wang,Shengjie Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出STAR框架，通过语义对齐模块（TSA）和时序感知模块（STPR）解决少样本动作识别中的语义-时序错配和多尺度时序建模问题，在五个基准数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有少样本动作识别方法存在两个关键问题：1）静态文本提示无法捕捉稀疏分布于视频序列中的关键视觉线索，导致语义-时序错配；2）短时判别性线索和长程依赖关系常被过度平滑或碎片化，难以对多尺度时序动态进行充分建模。

Method: STAR框架包含两个核心模块：1）语义对齐模块，提出时序语义注意力（TSA）机制，实现帧级跨模态对齐以保持细粒度语义-时序一致性；2）时序感知模块，提出语义时序原型精炼器（STPR），将语义引导的Mamba块与多频时序采样、双向状态空间精炼相结合，生成语义对齐且判别性强的原型。此外，利用大语言模型生成时序依赖的类别描述符提供长程语义引导。

Result: 在五个少样本动作识别基准数据集上均取得SOTA结果。例如，在SSv2-Full和SSv2-Small数据集上1-shot设置下分别提升8.1%和6.7%，在HMDB51上提升7.3%。

Conclusion: STAR框架通过语义-时序自适应表示学习，有效弥合了语义和时序两方面的差距，验证了在有限监督下的有效性。

Abstract: Few-shot action recognition (FSAR) requires models to generalize to novel action categories from only a handful of annotated samples. Despite progress with vision-language models, existing approaches still suffer from semantic-temporal misalignment, where static textual prompts fail to capture decisive visual cues that appear sparsely across sequences, and from inadequate modeling of multi-scale temporal dynamics, as short-term discriminative cues and long-range dependencies are often either oversmoothed or fragmented. To address these challenges, we propose Semantic Temporal Adaptive Representation Learning (STAR), a unified framework, consisting of a semantic-alignment component and a temporal-aware component, effectively bridging the semantic and temporal gaps and transferring the sequence modeling capability of Mamba into the FSAR. The semantic alignment module introduces a Temporal Semantic Attention (TSA) mechanism, which performs frame-level cross-modal alignment with textual cues, ensuring fine-grained semantic-temporal consistency. The temporal-aware module incorporates a Semantic Temporal Prototype Refiner (STPR) that integrates semantic-guided Mamba blocks with multi-frequency temporal sampling and bidirectional state-space refinement, yielding semantically aligned prototypes with enhanced discriminative fidelity and temporal consistency. Furthermore, temporally dependent class descriptors derived from large language models (LLMs) provide long-range semantic guidance. Extensive experiments on five FSAR benchmarks demonstrate the consistent superiority of STAR over state-of-the-art methods. For instance, STAR achieves up to 8.1% and 6.7% gains on the SSv2-Full and SSv2-Small datasets under the 1-shot setting, and 7.3% on HMDB51, validating its effectiveness under limited supervision. The code is available at https://github.com/HongliLiu1/STAR-main.

</details>


### [59] [Skill-Aligned Annotation for Reliable Evaluation in Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13223)
*Abdelrahman Eldesokey,Merey Ramazanova,Ahmad Sait,Ansar Khangeldin,Karen Sanchez,Tong Zhang,Bernard Ghanem*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出面向技能对齐标注的文本到图像生成评估框架，通过为不同评估技能匹配差异化标注策略（而非统一标注方式），提高了评估的一致性和稳定性，并实现了可扩展的自动化评估流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2I评估对不同技能的评估任务（如Likert量表、二元问答）采用统一标注机制，忽视了任务本质差异，导致评估信号不一致。

Method: 提出技能对齐标注方法，根据各评估技能的底层特征设计差异化标注策略；系统比较了技能对齐标注与统一基线；构建自动化评估管线实现细粒度、空间可定位的评估。

Result: 技能对齐标注相比统一标注产生更一致的评估信号，具有更高的标注者间一致性和跨模型稳定性。

Conclusion: 改进评估基础（而非简单扩大标注规模）能有效提升评估可靠性与效率，呼吁将评估协议优化作为模型评估的核心方向。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) generation has advanced rapidly, making reliable evaluation critical as performance differences between models narrow. Existing evaluation practices typically apply uniform annotation mechanisms, such as Likert-scale or binary question answering (BQA), across heterogeneous evaluation skills, despite fundamental differences in their nature. In this work, we revisit T2I evaluation through the lens of skill-aligned annotation, where annotation strategies reflect the underlying characteristics of each evaluation skill. We systematically compare skill-aligned annotation against uniform baselines and show that it produces more consistent evaluation signals, with higher inter-annotator agreement and improved stability across models. Finally, we present an automated pipeline that instantiates the proposed evaluation protocol, enabling scalable and fine-grained evaluation with spatially grounded feedback. Our work highlights that improving the foundations of image evaluation can increase reliability and efficiency without simply scaling annotation effort. We hope this motivates further research on refining evaluation protocols as a central component of reliable model assessment.

</details>


### [60] [ReTool-Video: Recursive Tool-Using Video Agents with Meta-Augmented Tool Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13228)
*Xiao Liu,Nayu Liu,Junnan Zhu,Ruirui Chen,Guohui Xiang,Changjian Wang,Kaiwen Wei,Rongzhen Li,Jiang Zhong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MetaAug-Video工具库（134个工具，含26个基础工具和108个元工具）和ReTool-Video递归工具使用方法，通过细粒度操作和分层动作空间，在MVBench、MLVU和Video-MME基准上显著提升视频理解性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频智能体面临两个限制：(1) 粗粒度的工具空间缺乏细粒度操作，难以支持组合推理；(2) 扁平的动作空间迫使高层视频意图直接映射为原始工具调用，缺乏中间抽象层。

Method: 提出两个互补设计：(1) MVTL工具库，包含26个基础工具（多模态信号处理）和108个元工具（过滤、聚合、重排序、格式化等中间结果操作），支持双层访问结构化视频信息和原始模态证据；(2) ReTool-Video递归工具使用方法，匹配的动作直接执行，未匹配的意图委托给解析器进行参数修复、工具替换或分解，将抽象动作逐步转化为具体多模态操作。

Result: 在MVBench、MLVU和Video-MME（无字幕）基准上，ReTool-Video一致优于强基线方法。进一步分析表明，递归接地和细粒度元工具提升了复杂视频理解的稳定性和有效性。

Conclusion: 细粒度的元工具库与递归工具使用策略相结合，能有效解决现有视频智能体在组合推理和动作空间上的局限，为复杂视频理解提供更稳定有效的方案。

Abstract: Video understanding requires active evidence seeking, motivating tool-augmented video agents for temporal reasoning, cross-modal understanding, and complex question answering. Existing video agents have improved video reasoning with retrieval, memory, frame inspection, and verifier tools, but they still face two limitations: (1) a coarse tool space that lacks fine-grained operations for compositional reasoning; and (2) a flat action space that forces high-level video intents into primitive executable tool calls. In this paper, we address these challenges with two complementary designs. First, we construct a MetaAug-Video Tool Library (MVTL), an extensible tool library with 134 registered tools, including 26 base tools for general multimodal signal processing and 108 meta tools for filtering, aggregation, reranking, formatting, and other intermediate-result operations. MVTL supports dual-level access to both structured video information and raw modal evidence, enabling diverse video reasoning scenarios. Second, we propose ReTool-Video, a recursive tool-using method that grounds high-level video intents into executable tool chains. In ReTool-Video, matched actions are executed directly, while unmatched intents are delegated to a resolver for parameter repair, tool substitution, or decomposition. This allows abstract actions such as temporal merging, cross-modal verification, or repeated-event aggregation to be progressively translated into concrete multimodal operations at runtime. Experiments on MVBench, MLVU, and Video-MME w/o sub. show that ReTool-Video consistently outperforms strong baselines. Further analysis demonstrates that recursive grounding and fine-grained meta tools improve the stability and effectiveness of complex video understanding.

</details>


### [61] [X-Restormer++: 1st Place Solution for the UG2+ CVPR 2026 All-Weather Restoration Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13258)
*Youwei Pan,Leilei Cao,Yingfang Zhu,Fengjie Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于X-Restormer框架的全天候图像恢复方法，通过集成空间自适应输入缩放、梯度引导边缘感知损失以及大规模数据扩充，在CVPR 2026 UG2+挑战赛Track 1中获得第一名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全天候图像恢复方法在处理复杂退化场景时，对空间结构细节和边缘信息的保持能力不足，且训练数据有限，限制了模型在多样化天气条件下的泛化性能。

Method: 基于X-Restormer双注意力框架（MDTA和OCA），集成Restormer-Plus的空间自适应输入缩放机制；提出梯度引导边缘感知损失（GGEA loss），与L1和MS-SSIM损失联合优化；额外引入24,500对来自FoundIR和WeatherBench的退化-干净图像对进行数据扩充。

Result: 该方法在CVPR 2026 UG2+挑战赛Track 1中取得第一名。

Conclusion: 空间自适应缩放、GGEA损失和大规模数据扩充策略有效提升了全天候图像恢复的性能，证明了结合多尺度结构保持和边缘感知学习的有效性。

Abstract: In this work, we present our winning solution for the 8th UG2+ Challenge (CVPR 2026) Track 1: Image Restoration under All-weather Conditions. Our method is built upon the strong baseline framework X-Restormer, which effectively captures both channel-wise global dependencies and spatially-local structural information through its dual-attention design (Multi-DConv Head Transposed Attention and Overlapping Cross-Attention). To further boost the restoration performance, we propose several key improvements. First, we integrate the spatially-adaptive input scaling mechanism from Restormer-Plus to dynamically adjust the spatial weights of the input image, enhancing spatial adaptability. Second, to better preserve structural details and edge information, we introduce a novel Gradient-Guided Edge-Aware (GGEA) loss, which is combined with L1 and Multi-Scale SSIM losses in a unified training objective. Third, we significantly expand the training data by incorporating an extra 24,500 degraded-clean image pairs from FoundIR and WeatherBench alongside the original WeatherStream dataset. With these strategies, our proposed method successfully ranks the 1st place in the challenge.

</details>


### [62] [Img2CADSeq: Image-to-CAD Generation via Sequence-Based Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13293)
*Shiyu Tan,Zixuan Zhao,Hao Gao,Zhiheng Chen,Xiaolong Yin,Enya Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Img2CADSeq提出了一种多阶段流水线，通过三级层次码本编码CAD序列，利用重要性优先级策略（轮廓优先于细节），结合粗到细的点云中间表示和对比学习对齐2D视觉与3DCAD序列，并引入CAD-220K和PrintCAD数据集，在单视图图像重建高质量BRep任务上显著超越现有方法，可直接生成标准STEP文件用于商业CAD软件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单视图图像重建高质量的边界表示(BRep)具有挑战性，主要由于拓扑约束和操作序列的复杂性，现有方法难以生成符合工业标准且可直接使用的CAD模型。

Method: 1) 将CAD序列编码为三级层次码本；2) 采用重要性优先级策略（轮廓优先于细节）压缩长序列到稳定的离散潜空间；3) 利用粗到细的点云中间表示桥接模态差异；4) 通过对比学习对齐2D视觉特征与3D CAD序列；5) 使用VQ-Diffusion模型进行条件生成；6) 引入CAD-220K和PrintCAD数据集增强工业域适应性。

Result: 在多项实验中显著超越现有最优方法，生成的STEP文件可直接在商业CAD软件中使用。

Conclusion: 该方法的层次码本编码和重要性优先级策略有效解决了CAD序列的拓扑约束和长序列压缩问题，多模态对齐策略成功弥合了2D图像到3D CAD的模态鸿沟，且具备良好的工业域适应性。

Abstract: Boundary Representation (BRep) is the standard format for Computer-Aided Design (CAD), yet reconstructing high-quality BReps from single-view images remains challenging due to the complexity of topological constraints and operation sequences. We present Img2CADSeq, a multi-stage pipeline that overcomes these limitations by encoding CAD sequences into a three-level hierarchical codebook. Guided by an importance prioritization, this strategy values profiles over details, compressing long sequences into a stable discrete latent space. To bridge the modality gap, we leverage a coarse-to-fine point cloud intermediate, aligning 2D visual features with 3D CAD sequences via contrastive learning to condition a VQ-Diffusion model. Supported by newly introduced CAD-220K and PrintCAD datasets, our approach ensures robust industrial domain adaptation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Img2CADSeq significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods, producing standard STEP files that can be directly used in commercial CAD software.

</details>


### [63] [Color Constancy in Hyperspectral Imaging via Reduced Spectral Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13306)
*G. Dofri Vidarsson,Liying Lu,Sabine Süsstrunk*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了超光谱成像中光谱维度与表示选择对光照估计性能的影响，基于Color-by-Correlation框架分析不同降维策略，发现紧凑光谱表示在特定条件下优于传统RGB方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有光照估计方法大多基于三通道RGB图像，受限于有限的频谱信息；超光谱成像虽然能提供更丰富的场景辐射表示，但其高维度带来计算和统计上的挑战。本文旨在系统研究光谱维度和表示选择对光照估计性能的影响。

Method: 采用Color-by-Correlation框架作为估计骨干，针对超光谱数据实施不同的光谱维度降维策略，系统分析光谱维度与表示选择对光照估计性能的影响。

Result: 紧凑的光谱表示在特定条件下能够超越传统RGB方法的光照估计性能，为如何高效利用超光谱信息提供了实用见解。

Conclusion: 超光谱成像中合理选择光谱维度和降维策略可以缓解光照估计中的模糊性，紧凑光谱表示在效率与性能间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Illuminant estimation aims to infer scene illumination from image measurements despite intrinsic ambiguities between surface reflectance and lighting. Most existing methods operate on trichromatic RGB images and are therefore fundamentally limited by the restricted spectral information available. Hyperspectral imaging provides a much richer representation of scene radiance and has the potential to alleviate these ambiguities. However, its high dimensionality poses computational and statistical challenges. In this work, we systematically study the effect of spectral dimensionality and representation choice on illuminant estimation performance using hyperspectral data. We adopt the practical and effective Color-by-Correlation (CbC) framework as the estimation backbone and analyze its behavior under different spectral dimensionality reduction strategies. Our results offer practical insights into how hyperspectral information can be efficiently exploited for illuminant estimation and identify conditions under which compact spectral representations outperform conventional RGB-based approaches. The code is available at https://github.com/IVRL/Reduced-Spectral-Color-Constancy.

</details>


### [64] [Test-time Sparsity for Extreme Fast Action Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13316)
*Kangye Ji,Yuan Meng,Jianbo Zhou,Ye Li,Chen Tang,Zhi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出测试时稀疏性（Test-time Sparsity）方法，通过动态预测可剪枝的残差计算来加速动作扩散模型。针对两大瓶颈：1) 重复条件编码和剪枝抵消加速效果，设计了高度并行化推理管线，将编码与剪枝从去噪循环中解耦并异步执行；2) 缓存特征无法约束大剪枝误差，提出全向复用策略，选择性复用来自当前前向、先前去噪步和早期 rollout 迭代的特征。实验表明，该方法减少92% FLOPs，实现5倍加速，无损性能下推理频率达47.5 Hz。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作扩散模型虽能生成高保真动作，但迭代去噪过程计算成本高昂。现有加速技术（如缓存特征复用）难以适应开放环境中多样感知和多轮 rollout 迭代带来的策略动态变化。因此需要一种能在测试时动态预测可剪枝计算的方法来加速动作扩散。

Method: 1) 设计轻量级剪枝器，与扩散Transformer共享编码器；2) 将所有去噪时间步并行处理，将编码和剪枝从自回归去噪循环中解耦；3) 通过异步机制将剪枝器与解码器前向推理重叠；4) 提出全向复用策略，从当前前向、先前去噪时间步和早期 rollout 迭代中缓存特征，实现95%稀疏度；5) 采样少量动作轨迹逐步监督稀疏化扩散过程。

Result: FLOPs减少92%，动作生成加速5倍，实现无损性能，推理频率达到47.5 Hz。

Conclusion: 提出的测试时稀疏性方法有效克服了动作扩散加速中的两大瓶颈，通过并行化推理管线设计和全向特征复用策略，在极高稀疏度下仍保持生成质量，显著提升了动作扩散模型的推理效率。

Abstract: Action diffusion excels at high-fidelity action generation but incurs heavy computational costs owing to its iterative denoising nature. Despite current technologies showing promise in accelerating diffusion transformers by reusing the cached features, they struggle to adapt to policy dynamics arising from diverse perceptions and multi-round rollout iterations in open environments. We propose test-time sparsity to tackle this challenge, which aims to accelerate action diffusion by dynamically predicting prunable residual computations for each model forward at test time. However, two bottlenecks remain in this paradigm: 1) repetitive conditional encoding and pruning offset most potential speed gains, and 2) the features cached from previous denoising timesteps cannot constrain large pruning errors under aggressive sparsity. To address the first bottleneck, we design a highly parallelized inference pipeline that minimizes the non-decoder delay to milliseconds. Specifically, we first design a lightweight pruner that shares the encoder with the diffusion transformer. Then, we decouple the encoding and pruning from the autoregressive denoising loop by processing all denoising timesteps in parallel, and overlap the pruner with the decoder forward inference through asynchronism. To overcome the second bottleneck, we introduce an omnidirectional reusing strategy, which achieves 95% sparsity by selectively reusing features cached from the current forward, previous denoising timesteps, and earlier rollout iterations. To learn the rollout-level reusing strategies, we sample a few action trajectories to supervise the sparsified diffusion step by step. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method reduces FLOPs by 92% and accelerates action generation by 5x, achieving lossless performance with an inference frequency of 47.5 Hz. Our code is available at https://github.com/ky-ji/Test-time-Sparsity.

</details>


### [65] [KamonBench: A Grammar-Based Dataset for Evaluating Compositional Factor Recovery in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13322)
*Richard Sproat,Stefano Peluchetti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了KamonBench，一个基于语法的图像-结构基准，包含20,000个合成日本家纹及其结构化描述，用于评估视觉-语言模型在稀疏组合视觉识别和因子恢复方面的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日本家纹（Kamon）是组合视觉识别的天然测试案例——每个家纹由少量符号选择组合而成，但可能的描述空间是稀疏的。现有方法缺乏对这种稀疏组合结构进行精细评估的基准。

Method: 构建了包含20,000个合成家纹的KamonBench数据集，每个家纹以"kamon yōgo"描述语言、日语分析、英文翻译和程序代码四种形式标注。图像由容器(container)、修饰符(modifier)和主题(motif)三个已知因子生成，支持程序代码因子度量、因子对重组拆分、反事实主题敏感性组和线性探针等评估方法。

Result: 提供了ViT编码器/Transformer解码器和两种VGG n-gram解码器（带/不带学习位置掩码）的基线结果。

Conclusion: KamonBench为稀疏组合视觉识别和因子恢复提供了受控测试平台，支持超越字幕级别的细粒度评估。

Abstract: Kamon (family crests) are an important part of Japanese culture and a natural test case for compositional visual recognition: each crest combines a small number of symbolic choices, but the space of possible descriptions is sparse. We introduce KamonBench, a grammar-based image-to-structure benchmark with 20,000 synthetic composite crests and auxiliary component examples. Each composite crest is paired with a formal kamon description language - "kamon yōgo" - description, a segmented Japanese analysis, an English translation, and a non-linguistic program code. Because each synthetic crest is generated from known factors, namely container, modifier, and motif, KamonBench supports evaluation beyond caption-level accuracy: direct program-code factor metrics, controlled factor-pair recombination splits, counterfactual motif-sensitivity groups under fixed container-modifier contexts, and linear probes of factor accessibility. We include baseline results for a ViT encoder/Transformer decoder and two VGG n-gram decoders, with and without learned positional masks. KamonBench therefore provides a controlled testbed for sparse compositional visual recognition and factor recovery in vision-language models.

</details>


### [66] [Drag within Prior Distribution: Text-Conditioned Point-Based Image Editing within Distribution Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13349)
*Haoyang Hu,Masataka Seo,Yen-Wei Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于扩散模型的点编辑方法，通过CLIP引导、先验保持损失和方向加权点跟踪机制，分别解决全局编辑中的语义对齐问题、分布偏移问题和细粒度编辑中的跟踪精度问题，从而提升编辑质量和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于扩散模型的点编辑方法存在以下局限：(1) 依赖处理点与目标点对定义运动轨迹，引入歧义或不必要改动；(2) 当点对距离较大时，累积扰动导致噪声隐变量偏离反演得分轨迹，产生不自然伪影。

Method: (1) 引入CLIP模型评估和指导中间编辑步骤，确保全局编辑的语义对齐；(2) 提出先验保持损失，约束优化后的隐变量保持在扩散先验的采样空间内，增强与原始数据分布的一致性；(3) 提出方向加权点跟踪机制，在相似特征区域内引导编辑过程朝向目标方向，提高跟踪精度和生成质量，同时减少编辑时间。

Result: 在全局编辑任务中，CLIP引导和先验保持损失有效解决了语义对齐和分布偏移问题；在细粒度编辑任务中，方向加权点跟踪机制提升了跟踪精度和生成质量，并缩短了编辑时间。

Conclusion: 所提方法通过语义引导、分布约束和方向加权跟踪三大模块，克服了传统点编辑方法的歧义性和伪影问题，在全局和细粒度编辑任务中均取得了更优的性能。

Abstract: Diffusion-based point editing methods have gained significant traction in image editing tasks due to their ability to manipulate image semantics and fine details by applying localized perturbations on the manifold of noise latent. However, these approaches face several limitations. Traditional point-based editing relies on pairs of handle and target points to define motion trajectories, which can introduce ambiguity or unnecessary alterations. Furthermore, when the distance between the handle and target points is large, the accumulated perturbations often cause the noise latent deviation from inversion score trajectory, resulting in unnatural artifacts. To address these issues in global editing tasks, we introduce a CLIP-based model to evaluate and guide intermediate editing steps, ensuring that the generated results remain both semantically aligned. Additionally, we propose a prior-preservation loss that constrains the optimized latent code to stay within the sampling space of the diffusion prior, improving consistency with the original data distribution, to ensure the model generates images along a familiar score trajectory. For fine-grained tasks, we present a directionally-weighted point tracking mechanism that steers the editing process toward the target direction within similar feature regions. This improves both the tracking accuracy and generation quality, while also reducing the editing time.

</details>


### [67] [Neural Surrogate Forward Modelling For Electrocardiology Without Explicit Intracellular Conductivity Tensor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13366)
*Shaheim Ogbomo-Harmitt,Cesare Magnetti,Jakub Grzelak,Oleg Aslanidi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度学习的直接映射方法，无需显式输入细胞内电导率张量，即可从左心房细胞内电位生成远场心电图，在仅74名受试者训练下实现了R²=0.949±0.037，有望减少结构不确定性并改善房颤的无创评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于物理的前向模型需要显式指定细胞内电导率张量，而该参数在临床实践中无法直接测量，且会引入结构性建模误差。因此，研究旨在开发一种无需该输入即可准确建模心脏电信道的方法。

Method: 采用深度学习模型，直接从左心房细胞内电位学习到远场心电图的映射关系，隐式地学习电生理传播过程，避免了显式建模细胞内电导率的需求。

Result: 尽管仅使用74名受试者进行训练，模型达到了R²=0.949±0.037的优异性能，表明该方法具有很高的准确性。

Conclusion: 该深度学习前向建模方法有潜力减少结构性不确定性，改善房颤的无创评估，为临床心脏电生理提供更实用的工具。

Abstract: Accurate forward modelling is essential for non-invasive cardiac electrophysiology, particularly in atrial fibrillation, where electrical activation is highly disorganised. Conventional physics-based forward models require explicit specification of intracellular conductivity tensors, which are not directly measurable in clinical practice and introduce structural modelling errors. This proof-of-concept study presents a deep learning approach that learns a direct mapping from left atrial intracellular electrical potentials to far-field ECGs without requiring explicit intracellular conductivity inputs at inference time. Despite training only on 74 subjects, the model achieved an R2 of 0.949 \pm 0.037, highlighting potential to reduce structural uncertainty and improve non-invasive AF assessment.

</details>


### [68] [GRIP-VLM: Group-Relative Importance Pruning for Efficient Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13375)
*Mingzhe Huang,Weijun Wang,Xin Ding,Liang Mi,Hao Wen,Yuanchun Li,Lichen Pang,Shansong Yang,Yunxin Liu,Ting Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对VLM中视觉token剪枝的离散组合优化难题，提出基于强化学习（GRPO）的GRIP-VLM框架，通过将剪枝建模为马尔可夫决策过程并引入有监督预热和预算感知评分器，在保持精度的前提下实现高达15%的推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于连续梯度松弛的训练感知剪枝方法在处理视觉token剪枝这一离散、非凸组合问题时，容易陷入次优局部极小值，尤其是在激进的压缩预算下性能退化严重。

Method: 将token剪枝形式化为马尔可夫决策过程，采用组相对策略优化（GRPO）结合有监督预热直接探索离散选择空间；设计预算感知评分器轻量级评估每个token的重要性，无需重训即可适配任意压缩比。

Result: 在多种多模态基准上，GRIP-VLM一致优于启发式和监督学习基线，实现了更优的帕累托前沿，在同等精度下推理速度提升高达15%。

Conclusion: 基于强化学习的离散剪枝策略比连续梯度松弛方法更适合视觉token剪枝任务，能够有效克服局部最优问题，在压缩效率和模型性能之间取得更优权衡。

Abstract: In Vision-Language Models (VLMs), processing a massive number of visual tokens incurs prohibitive computational overhead. While recent training-aware pruning methods attempt to selectively discard redundant tokens, they largely rely on continuous-gradient relaxations. However, visual token pruning is inherently a discrete, non-convex combinatorial problem; consequently, these continuous approximations frequently trap the optimization in sub-optimal local minima, especially under aggressive compression budgets. To overcome this fundamental bottleneck, we propose GRIP-VLM, a Group-Relative Importance Pruning framework driven by Reinforcement Learning. Rather than relying on smooth-gradient assumptions, GRIP-VLM formulates pruning as a Markov Decision Process, employing a Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) paradigm anchored by supervised warm-up to directly explore the discrete selection space. Integrated with a budget-aware scorer, our lightweight agent dynamically evaluates per-token importance and adapts to arbitrary compression ratios without retraining. Extensive experiments across diverse multimodal benchmarks demonstrate that GRIP-VLM consistently outperforms heuristic and supervised-learning baselines, achieving a superior Pareto frontier and delivering up to a 15\% inference speedup at equal accuracy.

</details>


### [69] [PreFIQs: Face Image Quality Is What Survives Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13396)
*Jan Niklas Kolf,Guray Ozgur,Andrea Atzori,Žiga Babnik,Vitomir Štruc,Naser Damer,Fadi Boutros*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PreFIQs提出了一种基于剪枝识别样例(PIE)假设的无监督、无需训练的FIQA框架，通过衡量原始模型与剪枝后模型在人脸嵌入上的欧氏距离来评估图像质量，在多个基准上取得了竞争性或最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FIQA方法通常需要监督训练或复杂计算，本文旨在探索一种无需训练、高效且理论上可解释的质量评估方法。

Method: 基于PIE假设，利用预训练FR模型及其剪枝版本提取L2归一化嵌入，计算两者之间的欧氏距离作为质量分数；并通过雅可比-向量积分析提供一阶理论解释。

Result: 在8个基准和4种FR模型上，PreFIQs达到与SOTA方法竞争甚至更优的性能，在多数据集上刷新了SOTA纪录。

Conclusion: 参数稀疏化可作为人脸图像效用的原则性且高效信号，图像质量本质上是在剪枝中存活下来的东西。

Abstract: Face Image Quality Assessment (FIQA) evaluates the utility of a face image for automated face recognition (FR) systems. In this work, we propose PreFIQs, an unsupervised and training-free FIQA framework grounded in the Pruning Identified Exemplar (PIE) hypothesis. We hypothesize that low-utility face images rely disproportionately on fragile network parameters, resulting in larger geometric displacement of their embeddings under model sparsification. Accordingly, PreFIQs quantifies image utility as the Euclidean distance between L2-normalized embeddings extracted from a pre-trained FR model and its pruned counterpart. We provide a first-order theoretical justification via a Jacobian-vector product analysis, demonstrating that this empirical drift serves as a computationally efficient approximation of the exact geometric sensitivity of the latent embedding manifold. Extensive experiments across eight benchmarks and four FR models demonstrate that PreFIQs achieves competitive or superior performance compared to state-of-the-art FIQA methods, including establishing new state-of-the-art results on several benchmarks, without any training or supervision. These results validate parameter sparsification as a principled and practically efficient signal for face image utility, and demonstrate that quality is, in essence, what survives pruning.

</details>


### [70] [Fast and Compact Graph Cuts for the Boykov-Kolmogorov Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13402)
*Christian Møller Mikkelstrup,Anders Bjorholm Dahl,Philip Bille,Vedrana Andersen Dahl,Inge Li Gørtz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文从理论和实践角度重新审视了Boykov-Kolmogorov (BK)算法，将其时间复杂度分析改进为O(mn|C|)，并提出fcBK算法，时间复杂度为O(m|C|)。同时提出紧凑图表示，使得在128GB内存的机器上可处理超过10^9顶点和10^10边的图。实验表明该BK实现在基准数据集上是最快的，强调了内存高效实现的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BK算法广泛应用于计算机视觉中的最小s-t割问题，但对其时间复杂度的理论分析不够精确，现有实现在处理大规模图时存在内存瓶颈，需要更高效的理论分析和实现方案。

Method: 1) 改进了BK算法的时间复杂度分析至O(mn|C|)；2) 提出了fcBK算法（fast and compact BK），时间复杂度为O(m|C|)；3) 设计了紧凑图表示方法，大幅降低内存占用。

Result: fcBK算法时间复杂度为O(m|C|)；紧凑图表示使得在128GB内存机器上可处理10^9顶点和10^10边的大规模图；该实现在综合基准数据集上超越了所有现有BK实现。

Conclusion: 内存高效实现对于最小s-t割算法至关重要，fcBK算法在理论和实践上均优于原BK算法，代码已开源供进一步研究。

Abstract: Computing a minimum $s$-$t$ cut in a graph is a solution to a wide range of computer vision problems, and is often done using the Boykov-Kolmogorov (BK) algorithm. In this paper, we revisit the BK algorithm from both a theoretical and practical point of view. We improve the analysis of the time complexity of the BK algorithm to $O(mn|C|)$ and propose a new algorithm, the fast and compact BK (fcBK) algorithm, with a time complexity of $O(m|C|)$, where $m$, $n$, and $|C|$ are the number of edges, number of vertices, and the capacity of the cut, respectively. We additionally propose a compact graph representation that allows our implementation to find a minimum $s$-$t$ cut in a graph with upwards of $10^9$ vertices and $10^{10}$ edges on a machine with 128 GB of memory. We find our implementation of the BK algorithm to be the fastest available implementation of the BK algorithm when evaluating on a comprehensive set of benchmark datasets, highlighting the importance of memory-efficient implementations. We make our implementations publicly available for further research and implementation development within minimum $s$-$t$ cut algorithms.

</details>


### [71] [Bayesian In Vivo Tracking of Synapses using Joint Poisson Deconvolution and Diffeomorphic Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13455)
*Shashwat Kumar,Dominic M. Padova,Binish Narang,Gabrielle I. Coste,Austin R. Graves,Richard L. Huganir,Adam S. Charles,Michael I. Miller,Anuj Srivastava*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于模板的贝叶斯框架，用于对荧光标记突触的体内显微成像数据进行建模与估计，同时实现模板构建、去噪反卷积、荧光强度推断、组织运动校正和置信区域估计，解决了低信噪比、高散粒噪声、非线性运动和非平稳波动等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 突触是动态重组的亚微米结构，纵向活体成像是研究突触可塑性及神经疾病机制的关键手段。然而，双光子显微成像受限于低激光功率，导致低信噪比、高散粒噪声、组织非线性运动、荧光非平稳波动及PSF模糊等问题，使得突触检测与追踪极为困难，尤其是在突触高密度区域。

Method: 提出一个统一的贝叶斯框架，将突触建模为在非线性组织变形下运动的可变亮度点源。后验分布整合了：用于域扭曲的微分同胚映射、用于成像过程的高斯点扩散函数、以及用于原始光子计数的泊松观测模型。该贝叶斯解同时实现了五项任务：概率模板构建、图像去噪与反卷积、荧光强度推断、微分同胚图像配准以及参数估计的置信区域计算。

Result: 在2D+t模拟数据集和3D+t纵向活体小鼠突触显微数据集上验证了该框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 该模板化贝叶斯框架为高密度突触的体内成像分析提供了统一的概率求解方案，能够同时处理多项低信噪比下的估计与校正任务，具有应用于神经科学长期研究的潜力。

Abstract: Synapses are densely packed submicron structures that dynamically reorganize during learning and memory formation. Longitudinal \textit{in vivo} imaging of fluorescently tagged synaptic receptors offers a promising opportunity to study large-scale synaptic dynamics and how these processes are disrupted in neurological disease. However, in vivo imaging with 2-photon microscopy uses low laser power and therefore suffers from low signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) and high shot noise, nonlinear tissue motion between days, nonstationary fluctuations in synaptic fluorescence, and significant blur induced by the microscope point spread function (PSF). Together, these factors make it challenging to detect and track synapses, especially in regions with high synaptic density. This paper presents a novel template-based framework for modeling synapses as varying luminance point sources that move under a nonlinear tissue deformation. Taking a unified Bayesian approach, we apply this model to microscopy data by deriving a posterior that incorporates a diffeomorphic mapping for domain warping, a Gaussian point spread function for the imaging process, and a Poisson observation model for raw photon counts. The Bayesian solution simultaneously: (1) Constructs a probabilistic template of synapse locations, (2) denoises and deconvolves the image data, (3) infers fluorescence intensities, (4) performs diffeomorphic image registration to correct for tissue motion, and (5) provides confidence regions for these parameter estimates. We demonstrate the framework on both a 2D+t simulated dataset and a 3D+t longitudinal \textit{in vivo} microscopy dataset of fluorescent synapses imaged in a mouse over two weeks.

</details>


### [72] [Z-Order Transformer for Feed-Forward Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13465)
*Can Wang,Lei Liu,Wei Jiang,Dong Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Transformer和Z-order稀疏注意力机制的前馈式3D高斯泼溅方法，能够在单次前向传播中高效预测高斯属性、减少冗余基元，实现快速高质量的新视角合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统3DGS依赖缓慢的迭代优化过程，无法满足实时性要求；现有前馈方法虽能直接预测高斯属性，但存在高斯基元冗余和渲染质量不足的问题。

Method: 提出基于Transformer的架构，利用Z-order策略将无序高斯集合组织为空间连续序列，并通过稀疏注意力机制捕捉高斯间的空间和语义关系，同时自适应抑制冗余基元，保留关键结构细节。

Result: 实验表明该方法在减少高斯基元数量的同时，实现了快速且高质量的新视角合成。

Conclusion: Z-order引导的稀疏注意力机制能有效建模高斯上下文、压缩基元数量并保证渲染质量，为实时新视角合成提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) have enabled significant progress in photorealistic novel view synthesis. However, traditional 3DGS relies on a slow, iterative optimization process, which limits its use in scenarios demanding real-time results. To overcome this bottleneck, recent feed-forward methods aim to predict Gaussian attributes directly from images, but they often struggle with the redundancy of Gaussian primitives and rendering quality. In this work, we introduce a transformer-based architecture specifically designed for feed-forward Gaussian Splatting. Our key insight is that spatial and semantic relationships among Gaussians can be effectively captured through a sparse attention mechanism, enabled by a Z-order strategy that organizes the unstructured Gaussian set into a spatially coherent sequence. Furthermore, we incorporate this Z-order strategy to adaptively suppress redundancy while preserving critical structural details. This allows the transformer to efficiently model context, compress Gaussian primitives, and predict Gaussian attributes in a single forward pass. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves fast and high-quality novel view synthesis with fewer Gaussian primitives.

</details>


### [73] [FedHPro: Federated Hyper-Prototype Learning via Gradient Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13475)
*Huan Wang,Jun Shen,Haoran Li,Zhenyu Yang,Jun Yan,Ousman Manjang,Yanlong Zhai,Di Wu,Guansong Pang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对原型级联邦学习中全局原型更新导致语义漂移的问题，提出联邦超原型学习框架FedHPro，通过可学习的全局类别超原型（经梯度匹配从客户真实样本中提取语义）替代传统原型聚合，结合互对比学习（带客户端特定间隔）和一致性约束，提升跨客户端语义对齐及模型泛化性能，在多个异构场景下达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有原型级联邦学习通过原型级平均或细化更新全局原型，导致跨客户端语义漂移，全局信号对齐不良，影响模型泛化能力。
